```python
def test_env_interpolation_and_missing_env_raises(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    (tmp_path / "demo.yaml").write_text("root: ${DEMO_ROOT}/raw\n", encoding="utf-8")
    monkeypatch.setenv("DEMO_ROOT", "resource")
    assert load_config("demo", config_dir=tmp_path)["root"] == "resource/raw"
    monkeypatch.delenv("DEMO_ROOT")
    with pytest.raises(ConfigError):
        load_config("demo", config_dir=tmp_path)
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/config/test_config_loader.py::test_env_interpolation_default_and_shell_semantics`  (0.0045s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_env_interpolation_default_and_shell_semantics(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    (tmp_path / "demo.yaml").write_text("a: ${DEMO_KEY:-}\nb: ${DEMO_KEY:-fallback}\nc: x-${DEMO_KEY:-y}-z\n", encoding="utf-8")
    monkeypatch.delenv("DEMO_KEY", raising=False)
    assert load_config("demo", config_dir=tmp_path) == {"a": "", "b": "fallback", "c": "x-y-z"}
    monkeypatch.setenv("DEMO_KEY", "")  # set-but-empty counts as unset for ":-", like the shell
    assert load_config("demo", config_dir=tmp_path)["b"] == "fallback"
    monkeypatch.setenv("DEMO_KEY", "real")
    assert load_config("demo", config_dir=tmp_path) == {"a": "real", "b": "real", "c": "x-real-z"}
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/config/test_config_loader.py::test_llm_yaml_holds_no_literal_credential`  (0.0031s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_llm_yaml_holds_no_literal_credential(monkeypatch):
    raw = yaml.safe_load((CONFIG_DIR / "agent/llm.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))  # un-interpolated, as committed
    assert re.fullmatch(r"\$\{[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]*(:-[^}]*)?\}", raw["api_key"] or "${EMPTY:-}"), (
        "api_key must be an env reference like ${LLM_API_KEY:-}, never a literal secret"
    )
    monkeypatch.delenv("LLM_API_KEY", raising=False)
    assert load_config("agent/llm")["api_key"] == ""  # no secret required to load the config (local servers, CI)
    monkeypatch.setenv("LLM_API_KEY", "from-env")
    assert load_config("agent/llm")["api_key"] == "from-env"
```

**Calls into the code under test** (input -> output)

```text

    in : name = 'agent/llm'
    in : config_dir = None
    out: {'provider': 'openai_compatible', 'model': 'gpt-5.4', 'base_url': '', 'api_key': '', 'timeout_seconds': 180.0}
[2] load_config
    in : name = 'agent/llm'
    in : config_dir = None
    out: {'provider': 'openai_compatible', 'model': 'gpt-5.4', 'base_url': '', 'api_key': 'from-env', 'timeout_seconds': 180.0}
```

```python
def test_every_data_paths_key_is_a_datapaths_field_and_vice_versa():
    # keeps data_paths.yaml free of dead keys (a key nobody reads) and DataPaths free of undeclared dirs
    yaml_keys = set(load_config("path/data_paths")["paths"])
    fields = {f.name for f in dataclasses.fields(get_data_paths())} - {"tag"}
    assert yaml_keys == fields
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/config/test_config_loader.py::test_every_data_paths_dir_is_distinct_and_project`  (0.0043s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_every_data_paths_dir_is_distinct_and_project():
    paths = get_data_paths()
    dirs = [getattr(paths, f.name) for f in dataclasses.fields(paths) if f.name != "tag"]
    assert len(set(dirs)) == len(dirs)
    # All directories should be under the project root (or be the project root itself)
    # src_root is the project root ("."); other dirs should have it as ancestor
    for d in dirs:
        if d == paths.src_root:
            # src_root IS the project root
            continue
        assert paths.src_root in d.parents or paths.src_root == d
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/config/test_config_loader.py::test_config_cache_invalidation_on_file_change`  (0.0159s)

_Test that modifying a config file clears the LRU cache in _limits.py._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_config_cache_invalidation_on_file_change(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """Test that modifying a config file clears the LRU cache in _limits.py."""
    (tmp_path / "test_config.yaml").write_text("test:\n  value: 10\n", encoding="utf-8")

    # First load
    cfg1 = load_config("test_config", config_dir=tmp_path)
    assert cfg1["test"]["value"] == 10

    # Wait a bit to ensure mtime changes
    time.sleep(0.01)

    # Modify the file
    (tmp_path / "test_config.yaml").write_text("test:\n  value: 20\n", encoding="utf-8")

    # Second load should get the new value
    cfg2 = load_config("test_config", config_dir=tmp_path)
    assert cfg2["test"]["value"] == 20
```

```python
def test_config_schema_validation(tmp_path):
    """Test that Pydantic schema validation works for agent configs."""
    # Create agent subdirectory
    agent_dir = tmp_path / "agent"
    agent_dir.mkdir()

    # Valid config
    (agent_dir / "agent.yaml").write_text(
        "agent:\n  max_steps: 8\n  plan_retries: 1\n  reflect_rounds: 1\n  reflect_error_chars: 300\n"
        "  max_tokens: 2048\n  temperature: 0.0\n  type1:\n    candidate_multiplier: 5\n    min_candidates: 50\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    cfg = load_config("agent/agent", config_dir=tmp_path)
    assert cfg["agent"]["max_steps"] == 8

    # Invalid config (negative value)
    (agent_dir / "agent.yaml").write_text(
        "agent:\n  max_steps: -1\n  plan_retries: 1\n  reflect_rounds: 1\n  reflect_error_chars: 300\n"
        "  max_tokens: 2048\n  temperature: 0.0\n  type1:\n    candidate_multiplier: 5\n    min_candidates: 50\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    with pytest.raises(ConfigError, match="Config validation failed"):
        load_config("agent/agent", config_dir=tmp_path)
```

_Setting CONFIG_OVERLAY_PATH then calling a limits getter directly must not be stale._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_limits_refresh_on_env_set_then_getter(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """Setting CONFIG_OVERLAY_PATH then calling a limits getter directly must not be stale."""
    base = get_agent_limits().reflect_rounds
    overlay = _write_overlay(tmp_path, {
        "agent/agent": {"agent": {"reflect_rounds": base + 2}},
    })
    _use_overlay(monkeypatch, overlay)
    assert get_agent_limits().reflect_rounds == base + 2
    monkeypatch.delenv("CONFIG_OVERLAY_PATH")
    assert get_agent_limits().reflect_rounds == base
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/config/test_config_overlay.py::test_limits_refresh_on_equal_mtime_swap`  (0.0091s)

_Switching between two overlay files with identical mtimes must not serve stale limits._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_limits_refresh_on_equal_mtime_swap(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """Switching between two overlay files with identical mtimes must not serve stale limits."""
    a = _write_overlay(tmp_path, {"agent/agent": {"agent": {"reflect_rounds": 2}}}, "a.yaml")
    b = _write_overlay(tmp_path, {"agent/agent": {"agent": {"reflect_rounds": 3}}}, "b.yaml")
    # Force identical mtimes — signature must still differ via resolved path
    ts = os.stat(a).st_mtime_ns
    os.utime(b, ns=(ts, ts))

    _use_overlay(monkeypatch, a)
    assert get_agent_limits().reflect_rounds == 2
    _use_overlay(monkeypatch, b)
    assert get_agent_limits().reflect_rounds == 3
```

```python
def test_data_paths_layout_is_under_resource():
    paths = get_data_paths()
    assert paths.tag == "multi_domain"
    assert paths.raw_dir == PROJECT_ROOT / "resource" / "raw"
    assert paths.review_path("Video_Games").is_relative_to(paths.raw_dir)
    # meta_path has fallback logic: checks resource/metadata/ first, then resource/raw/meta_*.jsonl, then resource/raw/meta_*.jsonl.gz
    # Since resource/metadata/meta_Video_Games.jsonl doesn't exist, it falls back to resource/raw/meta_Video_Games.jsonl.gz
    assert paths.meta_path("Video_Games") == PROJECT_ROOT / "resource" / "raw" / "meta_Video_Games.jsonl.gz"
    assert paths.cleaned_path("Electronics") == PROJECT_ROOT / "resource/cleaned/Electronics/interactions.parquet"
    assert paths.filtered_path == PROJECT_ROOT / "resource/filtered/multi_domain/interactions.parquet"
    assert paths.mapped_dir == PROJECT_ROOT / "resource/mapped/multi_domain"
    assert paths.splits_dir == PROJECT_ROOT / "resource/splits/multi_domain"
    assert paths.split_path("train") == PROJECT_ROOT / "resource/splits/multi_domain/train.parquet"
```

```python
def test_config_cli_resolves_paths_from_datapaths_for_shell_scripts():
    def run(*args):
        out = subprocess.run(
            [sys.executable, "-m", "package.config", *args], capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=PROJECT_ROOT
        )
        return out.returncode, out.stdout.split()

    assert run("path:raw_dir") == (0, ["resource/raw"])
    assert run("path:filtered_path") == (0, ["resource/filtered/multi_domain/interactions.parquet"])
    assert run("--domain", "Video_Games", "path:splits_dir") == (0, ["resource/splits/Video_Games"])
    assert run("--domain", "Video_Games", "path:log_dir") == (0, ["experiments/logs/Video_Games"])
    assert run("path:experiments_dir") == (0, ["experiments"])
    assert run("path:review_path:Video_Games") == (0, [get_data_paths().review_path("Video_Games").relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT).as_posix()])
    assert run("path:meta_path:Video_Games") == (0, ["resource/raw/meta_Video_Games.jsonl.gz"])
    assert run("path:cleaned_path:Electronics") == (0, ["resource/cleaned/Electronics/interactions.parquet"])
    assert run("--domain", "Electronics", "path:split_path:train") == (0, ["resource/splits/Electronics/train.parquet"])
    # shell and Python must agree: the CLI is just a view of DataPaths

    assert run("path:nope")[0] != 0
    assert run("path:cleaned_path")[0] != 0  # needs an argument
    assert run("path:raw_dir:oops")[0] != 0  # takes none
    assert run("path:split_path:validation_typo")[0] != 0
```

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_review_resolver_supports_actual_layout_and_legacy_fallback(tmp_path):
    from dataclasses import replace
    paths = replace(get_data_paths(), raw_dir=tmp_path)
    assert paths.review_path("D") == tmp_path / "D.jsonl.gz"
    legacy = tmp_path / "D.jsonl.gz"
    legacy.write_bytes(b"test")
    assert paths.review_path("D") == legacy
    plain = tmp_path / "D.jsonl"
    plain.write_text("test")
    assert paths.review_path("D") == plain
    nested = tmp_path / "D" / "D.jsonl"
    nested.parent.mkdir()
    nested.write_text("test")
    assert paths.review_path("D") == nested
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/config/test_dependency_direction.py::test_dependency_direction`  (0.3765s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_dependency_direction():
    offenders = []
    for path in _py_files(PACKAGE_ROOT):
        owner = _owner_package(_dotted_module(path))
        if owner is None:
            continue
        rel = path.relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT).as_posix()
        for dotted, lineno in _imported_modules(path):
            for forbidden in FORBIDDEN_IMPORTS[owner]:
                if _is_under(dotted, forbidden) and not any(
                    rel == file and _is_under(dotted, allowed) for file, allowed in ALLOWED_EXCEPTIONS
                ):
                    offenders.append(f"{rel}:{lineno}: {owner} must not import {dotted}")
    assert not offenders, "dependency-direction violations:\n" + "\n".join(sorted(set(offenders)))
```

```python
def test_allowed_exceptions_are_still_needed():
    """An exception that nothing uses any more must be deleted, so the list cannot rot."""
    used = set()
    for path in _py_files(PACKAGE_ROOT):
        rel = path.relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT).as_posix()
        for dotted, _ in _imported_modules(path):
            used.update((rel, allowed) for file, allowed in ALLOWED_EXCEPTIONS if rel == file and _is_under(dotted, allowed))
    assert used == ALLOWED_EXCEPTIONS
```

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_merge_domains_prevents_id_collision(video_games_df, toys_games_df):
    """
    Test QUAN TRỌNG NHẤT: cả 2 domain đều có raw parent_asin='ASIN_X'.
    Nếu không namespace đúng, 2 item hoàn toàn khác nhau (1 video game,
    1 món đồ chơi) sẽ bị hệ thống hiểu nhầm là CÙNG 1 item.
    """
    tagged_vg = tag_domain(video_games_df, "Video_Games")
    tagged_tg = tag_domain(toys_games_df, "Toys_and_Games")
    merged = merge_domains([tagged_vg, tagged_tg])

    namespaced_ids = set(merged["parent_asin"])
    assert f"Video_Games{ITEM_ID_SEPARATOR}ASIN_X" in namespaced_ids
    assert f"Toys_and_Games{ITEM_ID_SEPARATOR}ASIN_X" in namespaced_ids
    # 2 item id namespaced phải KHÁC NHAU dù raw asin giống nhau
    assert f"Video_Games{ITEM_ID_SEPARATOR}ASIN_X" != f"Toys_and_Games{ITEM_ID_SEPARATOR}ASIN_X"
    # tổng số dòng đúng bằng tổng 2 domain (không mất/nhân đôi dữ liệu)
    assert len(merged) == len(video_games_df) + len(toys_games_df)
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/config/test_import_resolution.py::test_all_package_imports_have_existing_files`  (0.5405s)

_Ensure all package.* imports in package/, scripts/, tests/ point to existing files._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_all_package_imports_have_existing_files():
    """Ensure all package.* imports in package/, scripts/, tests/ point to existing files."""
    offenders = []

    for path in _py_files():
        try:
            tree = ast.parse(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            imports = _extract_imports(tree)

            for module_name, lineno in imports:
                # Only check package.* imports
                if module_name.startswith("package."):
                    if not _module_file_exists(module_name):
                        rel_path = path.relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT)
                        offenders.append(f"{rel_path}:{lineno}: from {module_name}")
        except SyntaxError:
            # Skip files with syntax errors (they'll be caught by other tests)
            continue

    assert not offenders, "Imports to non-existent package modules:\n" + "\n".join(offenders)
```

```python
def test_every_public_module_has_one_public_symbol_named_after_the_file():
    offenders = []
    for path in _py_files(PACKAGE_ROOT):
        if path.stem.startswith("_") or path.stem in SKIPPED_STEMS:
            continue
        symbols = _public_top_level(path)
        rel = path.relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT).as_posix()
        if len(symbols) != 1:
            offenders.append(f"{rel}: expected exactly 1 public class/function, found {symbols or 'none'}")
        elif _norm(symbols[0]) != _norm(path.stem):
            offenders.append(f"{rel}: public symbol {symbols[0]!r} does not match the file name")
    assert not offenders, "atomic-file / name-match violations:\n" + "\n".join(offenders)
```

```python
def test_public_names_are_unique_across_the_package():
    seen: dict[str, str] = {}
    duplicates = []
    for path in _py_files(PACKAGE_ROOT):
        if path.stem.startswith("_") or path.stem in SKIPPED_STEMS:
            continue
        for name in _public_top_level(path):
            rel = path.relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT).as_posix()
            if name in seen:
                duplicates.append(f"{name}: {seen[name]} and {rel}")
            seen.setdefault(name, rel)
    assert not duplicates, "duplicate public names:\n" + "\n".join(duplicates)
```

```python
def test_no_module_imports_a_private_module_of_another_folder():
    offenders = []
    for root in CHECKED_ROOTS:
        for path in _py_files(root):
            importer = _dotted_module(path)
            rel_path = path.relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT).as_posix()
            for dotted, lineno in _imported_modules(path):
                if not dotted.startswith("package.") or not _module_exists(dotted):
                    continue
                owner = _private_owner(dotted)
                if owner is None:
                    continue
                inside_owner = importer == owner or importer.startswith(owner + ".")
                if not inside_owner and (rel_path, dotted) not in PRIVATE_IMPORT_EXCEPTIONS:
                    offenders.append(f"{rel_path}:{lineno}: imports {dotted}")
    assert not offenders, "cross-folder imports of `_` modules:\n" + "\n".join(sorted(set(offenders)))
```

```python
def test_no_hardcoded_project_dirs_in_code():
    # Directories come from DataPaths (configs/path/data_paths.yaml). Docstrings/comments may mention them.
    offenders = []
    for path in _py_files():
        for node in ast.walk(ast.parse(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))):
            if isinstance(node, ast.Constant) and isinstance(node.value, str):
                v = node.value
                if v == "experiments" or v.startswith(("experiments/", "resource/")) or v == "resource":
                    offenders.append(f"{path.relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT)}:{node.lineno}: {v!r}")
    assert not offenders, "hard-coded project paths:\n" + "\n".join(offenders)
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/tools/evaluation/test_ranking_streaming.py::test_uninterrupted_and_resume_produce_identical_quality[candidates]`  (0.5232s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize("interrupt_stage", ["candidates", "scored:"])
def test_uninterrupted_and_resume_produce_identical_quality(environment,monkeypatch,interrupt_stage):
    base,paths,items = environment
    reference = launch(monkeypatch,base,"reference")
    original = RankingRunStore.append
    def interrupt(self,stream,batch):
        original(self,stream,batch)
        if stream.startswith(interrupt_stage) and self.count(stream)==1:
            raise RuntimeError("simulated power loss after commit")
    with monkeypatch.context() as patch:
        patch.setattr(RankingRunStore,"append",interrupt)
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match="power loss"):
            launch(patch,base,"resumed")
    result = launch(monkeypatch,base,"resumed",resume=True)
    for name in ("candidate_lists.jsonl","scored.jsonl"):
        a=[json.loads(l) for l in (reference/name).read_text(encoding="utf8").splitlines()]
        b=[json.loads(l) for l in (result/name).read_text(encoding="utf8").splitlines()]
        for rows in (a,b):
            for row in rows:
                for field in ("source_latency_seconds","ranking_latency_seconds","fetch_latency_seconds"):
                    row.pop(field,None)
        assert a==b
    m=json.loads((result/"metrics.json").read_text())
    assert sum(v["semantic_errors"] for v in m["tool_health"].values())==0
    # The stream summary reproduces existing batch metric implementations.
    rows=[json.loads(l) for l in (result/"scored.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()]
    for merge in script.MERGES:
        for segment in script.SEGMENTS:
            selected=[r for r in rows if r["merge"]==merge and r["segment"]==segment and r["targets_unseen_in_fit"]]
            old=script._ranking_metrics([[items[i] for i in r["ranked_ids"]] for r in selected],
                                       [set(r["targets_unseen_in_fit"]) for r in selected],[1,3],len(items))
            assert old["metrics"]==m["ranking"][merge]["baseline"]["per_segment"][segment]["metrics"]
    # A finished checkpoint is idempotent and does not publish exp_002.
    launch(monkeypatch,base,"resumed",resume=True)
    assert not (result.parent/"exp_002").exists()
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/tools/evaluation/test_ranking_streaming.py::test_uninterrupted_and_resume_produce_identical_quality[scored:]`  (0.4319s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize("interrupt_stage", ["candidates", "scored:"])
def test_uninterrupted_and_resume_produce_identical_quality(environment,monkeypatch,interrupt_stage):
    base,paths,items = environment
    reference = launch(monkeypatch,base,"reference")
    original = RankingRunStore.append
    def interrupt(self,stream,batch):
        original(self,stream,batch)
        if stream.startswith(interrupt_stage) and self.count(stream)==1:
            raise RuntimeError("simulated power loss after commit")
    with monkeypatch.context() as patch:
        patch.setattr(RankingRunStore,"append",interrupt)
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match="power loss"):
            launch(patch,base,"resumed")
    result = launch(monkeypatch,base,"resumed",resume=True)
    for name in ("candidate_lists.jsonl","scored.jsonl"):
        a=[json.loads(l) for l in (reference/name).read_text(encoding="utf8").splitlines()]
        b=[json.loads(l) for l in (result/name).read_text(encoding="utf8").splitlines()]
        for rows in (a,b):
            for row in rows:
                for field in ("source_latency_seconds","ranking_latency_seconds","fetch_latency_seconds"):
                    row.pop(field,None)
        assert a==b
    m=json.loads((result/"metrics.json").read_text())
    assert sum(v["semantic_errors"] for v in m["tool_health"].values())==0
    # The stream summary reproduces existing batch metric implementations.
    rows=[json.loads(l) for l in (result/"scored.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()]
    for merge in script.MERGES:
        for segment in script.SEGMENTS:
            selected=[r for r in rows if r["merge"]==merge and r["segment"]==segment and r["targets_unseen_in_fit"]]
            old=script._ranking_metrics([[items[i] for i in r["ranked_ids"]] for r in selected],
                                       [set(r["targets_unseen_in_fit"]) for r in selected],[1,3],len(items))
            assert old["metrics"]==m["ranking"][merge]["baseline"]["per_segment"][segment]["metrics"]
    # A finished checkpoint is idempotent and does not publish exp_002.
    launch(monkeypatch,base,"resumed",resume=True)
    assert not (result.parent/"exp_002").exists()
```

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_frozen_candidates_and_weight_variants(environment,monkeypatch):
    base,paths,items = environment
    source = launch(monkeypatch,base,"source")
    def no_retrieval(*a,**k):
        raise AssertionError("frozen mode must not call SemanticSearch")
    monkeypatch.setattr(script.SearchTool,"execute",no_retrieval)
    result=launch(monkeypatch,base,"frozen",frozen=source,weights="0,1e-6")
    assert (source/"candidate_lists.jsonl").read_bytes()==(result/"candidate_lists.jsonl").read_bytes()
    m=json.loads((result/"metrics.json").read_text())
    assert set(m["ranking"]["union_rr"])=={"baseline","popularity_weight_0"}
    for merge in script.MERGES:
        for segment in script.SEGMENTS:
            a=json.loads((source/"metrics.json").read_text())["ranking"][merge]["baseline"]["per_segment"][segment]
            b=m["ranking"][merge]["baseline"]["per_segment"][segment]
            assert a["metrics"]==b["metrics"]
```

```python
def test_resume_after_export_failure_skips_models(environment,monkeypatch):
    base,paths,items = environment
    export = RankingRunStore.export
    def fail(self,streams,destination):
        export(self,streams,destination)
        if Path(destination).name == "scored.jsonl":
            raise RuntimeError("export interrupted")

        patch.setattr(RankingRunStore,"export",fail)
```

```python
def test_failed_baseline_confirmation_stops_tuning(environment,monkeypatch):
    base,paths,items = environment
    source=launch(monkeypatch,base,"bad_source")
    path=source/"metrics.json"
    metrics=json.loads(path.read_text())
    metrics["ranking"]["union_rr"]["baseline"]["per_segment"]["cold"]["metrics"]["1"]["ndcg@1"] += .1
    path.write_text(json.dumps(metrics))
    original=script.BaselineScorer
    called=[]
    def record(*a,**k):
        called.append(k["popularity_weight"])
        return original(*a,**k)
    monkeypatch.setattr(script,"BaselineScorer",record)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match="baseline confirmation failed"):
        launch(monkeypatch,base,"blocked_tuning",frozen=source,weights="0,1e-6")
    assert called==[1e-6]
    assert not (base/"blocked_tuning_output"/"exp_001").exists()
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/demo/test_live_backend.py::test_adapt_from_plan_executor_ok_status`  (0.0158s)

_Adapter phải chuyển kết quả PlanExecutor thành DemoResult với status='ok'._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_adapt_from_plan_executor_ok_status(corpus):
    """Adapter phải chuyển kết quả PlanExecutor thành DemoResult với status='ok'."""
    steps = [{"tool": "LookupTool", "args": {"item_ids": [VG_A]}}]
    executor = PlanExecutor(ScriptedLLM(plan(*steps)), [LookupTool(corpus)])
    results = executor.run(f"tell me about {VG_A}")

    request = DemoRequest(
        user_id="user123",
        text="test",
        reflect_rounds=0,
        use_memory=False,
        mode="planner",
    )

    item_id_to_metadata = {
        VG_A: {
            "domain": "Video_Games",
            "title": "Test Game",
            "store": "Amazon",
            "price": 49.99,
            "average_rating": 4.5,
            "rating_number": 100,
            "main_category": "Electronics",
        }
    }

    result = adapt_from_plan_executor(
        results=results,
        executor_last_plan=executor.last_plan,
        executor_last_question=executor.last_question,
        executor_last_reflections=executor.last_reflections,
        executor_last_first_attempt_ok=executor.last_first_attempt_ok,
        executor_last_truncated=executor.last_truncated,
        executor_last_usage=executor.last_usage,
        request=request,
        item_id_to_metadata=item_id_to_metadata,
    )

    assert result.status == "ok"
    assert result.text != ""
    assert isinstance(result.items, list)
    assert result.trace is not None
    assert result.trace.steps[0]["ok"] is True
    assert result.error is None
```

```python
def test_adapt_from_plan_executor_ask_user_status(corpus):
    """Adapter phải chuyển kết quả PlanExecutor với ask_user thành status='ask_user'."""
    executor = PlanExecutor(ScriptedLLM(plan(ask_user="Which category?")), [FilterTool(corpus)])
    results = executor.run("recommend something")

    request = DemoRequest(
        user_id="user123",
        text="test",
        reflect_rounds=0,
        use_memory=False,
        mode="planner",
    )

    result = adapt_from_plan_executor(
        results=results,
        executor_last_plan=executor.last_plan,
        executor_last_question=executor.last_question,
        executor_last_reflections=executor.last_reflections,
        executor_last_first_attempt_ok=executor.last_first_attempt_ok,
        executor_last_truncated=executor.last_truncated,
        executor_last_usage=executor.last_usage,
        request=request,
        item_id_to_metadata={},
    )

    assert result.status == "ask_user"
    assert result.trace is not None
    assert result.trace.ask_user == "Which category?"
```

```python
def test_adapt_from_plan_executor_no_result_status(corpus):
    """Adapter phải chuyển kết quả PlanExecutor không có item thành status='no_result'."""
    steps = [{"tool": "FilterTool", "args": {"limit": 0}}]
    # Cung cấp 2 replies để tránh lỗi pop từ empty list (nếu có reflect)
    executor = PlanExecutor(ScriptedLLM(plan(*steps), plan(*steps)), [FilterTool(corpus)], reflect_rounds=0)
    results = executor.run("list items")

    request = DemoRequest(
        user_id="user123",
        text="test",
        reflect_rounds=0,
        use_memory=False,
        mode="planner",
    )

    result = adapt_from_plan_executor(
        results=results,
        executor_last_plan=executor.last_plan,
        executor_last_question=executor.last_question,
        executor_last_reflections=executor.last_reflections,
        executor_last_first_attempt_ok=executor.last_first_attempt_ok,
        executor_last_truncated=executor.last_truncated,
        executor_last_usage=executor.last_usage,
        request=request,
        item_id_to_metadata={},
    )

    assert result.status == "no_result"
    assert len(result.items) == 0
```

```python
def test_adapt_from_plan_executor_error():
    """Adapter phải chuyển exception thành DemoResult với status='tool_error'."""
    request = DemoRequest(
        user_id="user123",
        text="test",
        reflect_rounds=0,
        use_memory=False,
        mode="planner",
    )

    result = adapt_from_plan_executor_error(ValueError("Invalid plan"), request)

    assert result.status == "tool_error"
    assert result.error is not None
    assert "ValueError" in result.error
    assert result.items == []
    assert result.trace is None
```

```python
def test_adapter_cuts_error_message():

        user_id="user123",
        text="test",
        reflect_rounds=0,
        use_memory=False,
        mode="planner",
    )

    assert len(result.error) <= 300  # MAX_ERROR_LENGTH
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/demo/test_live_backend.py::test_adapter_no_api_key_in_result`  (0.0076s)

_API key không được xuất hiện trong DemoResult/trace._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_adapter_no_api_key_in_result(corpus):
    """API key không được xuất hiện trong DemoResult/trace."""
    steps = [{"tool": "LookupTool", "args": {"item_ids": [VG_A]}}]
    executor = PlanExecutor(ScriptedLLM(plan(*steps)), [LookupTool(corpus)])
    results = executor.run(f"tell me about {VG_A}")

    request = DemoRequest(
        user_id="user123",
        text="test",
        reflect_rounds=0,
        use_memory=False,
        mode="planner",
    )

    result = adapt_from_plan_executor(
        results=results,
        executor_last_plan=executor.last_plan,
        executor_last_question=executor.last_question,
        executor_last_reflections=executor.last_reflections,
        executor_last_first_attempt_ok=executor.last_first_attempt_ok,
        executor_last_truncated=executor.last_truncated,
        executor_last_usage=executor.last_usage,
        request=request,
        item_id_to_metadata={VG_A: {"domain": "Video_Games", "title": "Test"}},
    )

    # Chuyển thành JSON string để kiểm tra
    result_str = json.dumps({
        "status": result.status,
        "text": result.text,
        "items": [i.__dict__ for i in result.items],
        "trace": result.trace.__dict__ if result.trace else None,
        "usage": result.usage,
        "error": result.error,
    })

    # Kiểm tra không có từ khóa liên quan API key
    assert "api_key" not in result_str.lower()
    assert "secret" not in result_str.lower()
    assert "token" not in result_str.lower() or "tokens" in result_str  # cho phép "tokens" (usage)
```

_LiveBackend.get_sample_users phải trả dict với 3 segment._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_live_backend_get_sample_users(corpus):
    """LiveBackend.get_sample_users phải trả dict với 3 segment."""
    from package.demo import LiveBackend
    from package.data.mapping import load_mappings
    from package.data.split import load_splits
    from package.config import get_data_paths

    # Dùng tag/default để test nhanh
    data_paths = get_data_paths()
    train, _, _ = load_splits(data_paths.splits_dir, ("user_idx", "item_idx", "timestamp"))
    user2id, _ = load_mappings(data_paths.mapped_dir)

    backend = LiveBackend()
    backend._fit_df = train
    backend._user_id_to_internal_id = user2id

    sample_dict = backend.get_sample_users(n_per_segment=2, sparse_max=5)

    assert isinstance(sample_dict, dict)
    assert set(sample_dict.keys()) == {"cold", "sparse", "warm"}
    # Số lượng user mỗi segment có thể ít hơn n_per_segment nếu không đủ user
    for segment, users in sample_dict.items():
        assert isinstance(users, list)
        assert len(users) <= 2

    # Also test that LiveBackend can be created with new parameters
    backend_with_params = LiveBackend(tag=data_paths.tag, eval_on="test", use_prebuilt=False, allow_missing_corpus_sources=True)
    assert backend_with_params._eval_on == "test"
    assert backend_with_params._use_prebuilt is False
    assert backend_with_params._allow_missing_corpus_sources is True
```

```python
def test_live_backend_get_profiles(corpus):
    """LiveBackend.get_profiles phải trả list DemoProfile."""
    from package.demo import LiveBackend, DemoProfile
    from package.data.mapping import load_mappings
    from package.data.split import load_splits
    from package.config import get_data_paths

    data_paths = get_data_paths()
    train, _, _ = load_splits(data_paths.splits_dir, ("user_idx", "item_idx", "timestamp"))
    user2id, _ = load_mappings(data_paths.mapped_dir)

    backend = LiveBackend()
    backend._fit_df = train
    backend._user_id_to_internal_id = user2id
    backend._initialized = True  # Bypass initialization check for test

    profiles = backend.get_profiles()

    assert isinstance(profiles, list)
    assert len(profiles) >= 1
    assert all(isinstance(p, DemoProfile) for p in profiles)

    # Check blank profile exists
    blank = next((p for p in profiles if p.profile_id == "blank"), None)
    assert blank is not None
    assert blank.user_id is None
    assert blank.segment == "blank"
```

```python
def test_live_backend_meta_files_uses_all_domains_when_none():
    """Khi không truyền domain, meta_files phải dùng tất cả domain từ config."""
    data_paths = get_data_paths()
    meta_files = build_meta_files(data_paths, None)
    # Check that meta_files is not empty
    assert len(meta_files) > 0
    # Check that all paths are strings
    for domain, path in meta_files:
        assert isinstance(domain, str)
        assert isinstance(path, str)
```

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize("reject_cache", [False, True])
def test_initialize_checks_dataset_bound_cache_and_records_refit(tmp_path, monkeypatch, corpus, cf_setup, reject_cache):
    from dataclasses import replace
    from package.demo.backend import live_backend as module
    from package.recommenders.runtime.item_knn_recommender import ItemKNNRecommender
    from package.recommenders.shared.provenance import fit_provenance
    from package.recommenders.shared._model_metadata import item_knn_data_hash
    interactions, user2id, item2id = cf_setup
    fit = interactions.assign(timestamp=1)
    empty = fit.iloc[:0]
    paths = replace(get_data_paths("synthetic"), item_knn_dir=tmp_path, corpus_dir=tmp_path)
    paths.item_knn_path("validation").touch()
    paths.corpus_path.touch()
    knn = ItemKNNRecommender(20).fit(fit)
    context = dict(user2id=user2id, item2id=item2id, eval_on="validation", tag="synthetic")
    knn._provenance = fit_provenance(fit, **context)
    knn._fit_data_hash = item_knn_data_hash(fit)
    loader = MagicMock(side_effect=ValueError("ItemKNN provenance mismatch")) if reject_cache else MagicMock(return_value=knn)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "get_data_paths", lambda *a, **k: paths)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "load_splits", lambda *a: (fit, empty, empty))
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "load_mappings", lambda *a: (user2id, item2id))
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "build_meta_files", lambda *a: [])
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "load_corpus", lambda *a, **k: (corpus, 1.))
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "load_item_knn", loader)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "build_llm_provider", lambda *a: MagicMock(health_check=lambda: True))
    backend = LiveBackend(tag="synthetic")
    backend.initialize()
    assert backend._initialized
    assert loader.call_args.kwargs == context
    assert backend._knn_provenance["status"] == ("refit_cache_rejected" if reject_cache else "validated_cache")
    assert backend._knn_provenance["data_hash"] == item_knn_data_hash(fit)
    assert backend._knn_provenance["fit_splits"] == ["train"]
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/demo/test_live_backend.py::test_initialize_checks_dataset_bound_cache_and_records_refit[True]`  (0.031s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize("reject_cache", [False, True])
def test_initialize_checks_dataset_bound_cache_and_records_refit(tmp_path, monkeypatch, corpus, cf_setup, reject_cache):
    from dataclasses import replace
    from package.demo.backend import live_backend as module
    from package.recommenders.runtime.item_knn_recommender import ItemKNNRecommender
    from package.recommenders.shared.provenance import fit_provenance
    from package.recommenders.shared._model_metadata import item_knn_data_hash
    interactions, user2id, item2id = cf_setup
    fit = interactions.assign(timestamp=1)
    empty = fit.iloc[:0]
    paths = replace(get_data_paths("synthetic"), item_knn_dir=tmp_path, corpus_dir=tmp_path)
    paths.item_knn_path("validation").touch()
    paths.corpus_path.touch()
    knn = ItemKNNRecommender(20).fit(fit)
    context = dict(user2id=user2id, item2id=item2id, eval_on="validation", tag="synthetic")
    knn._provenance = fit_provenance(fit, **context)
    knn._fit_data_hash = item_knn_data_hash(fit)
    loader = MagicMock(side_effect=ValueError("ItemKNN provenance mismatch")) if reject_cache else MagicMock(return_value=knn)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "get_data_paths", lambda *a, **k: paths)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "load_splits", lambda *a: (fit, empty, empty))
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "load_mappings", lambda *a: (user2id, item2id))
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "build_meta_files", lambda *a: [])
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "load_corpus", lambda *a, **k: (corpus, 1.))
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "load_item_knn", loader)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "build_llm_provider", lambda *a: MagicMock(health_check=lambda: True))
    backend = LiveBackend(tag="synthetic")
    backend.initialize()
    assert backend._initialized
    assert loader.call_args.kwargs == context
    assert backend._knn_provenance["status"] == ("refit_cache_rejected" if reject_cache else "validated_cache")
    assert backend._knn_provenance["data_hash"] == item_knn_data_hash(fit)
    assert backend._knn_provenance["fit_splits"] == ["train"]
```

```python
def test_format_trace_with_inference():
    """format_trace phải hiển thị thông tin inference khi có."""
    from package.demo import DemoTrace

    trace = DemoTrace(
        plan=[{"tool": "LookupTool"}],
        steps=[{"tool": "LookupTool", "ok": True, "error": None, "latency_seconds": 0.1}],
        ask_user=None,
        reflections=0,
        first_attempt_ok=True,
        truncated=False,
        usage={"attempts": 1, "prompt_tokens": 100, "completion_tokens": 50, "latency_seconds": 0.5},
        inference={
            "composer": "llm",
            "grounded": True,
            "fallback_reason": None,
            "reason": None,
            "usage": {"attempts": 1, "prompt_tokens": 200, "completion_tokens": 100, "latency_seconds": 1.0},
        },
    )

    formatted = format_trace(trace)

    assert "## Inference (tạo câu trả lời)" in formatted
    assert "Composer: llm" in formatted
    assert "Grounded: True" in formatted
    assert "Lần gọi LLM: 1" in formatted
    assert "Tokens: 200 + 100" in formatted
```

```python
def test_format_trace_without_inference():
    """format_trace không lỗi khi trace không có inference."""
    from package.demo import DemoTrace

    trace = DemoTrace(
        plan=[{"tool": "LookupTool"}],
        steps=[{"tool": "LookupTool", "ok": True, "error": None, "latency_seconds": 0.1}],
        ask_user=None,
        reflections=0,
        first_attempt_ok=True,
        truncated=False,
        usage={"attempts": 1, "prompt_tokens": 100, "completion_tokens": 50, "latency_seconds": 0.5},
        inference=None,
    )

    formatted = format_trace(trace)

    assert "## Inference (tạo câu trả lời)" not in formatted
    assert "## Plan JSON" in formatted
    assert "## Thực thi từng step" in formatted
```

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_pipeline_byte_level_equivalence():
    """Test that new pipeline produces deterministic outputs."""
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmpdir:
        tmp_root = Path(tmpdir)

        domains = ["DomainA", "DomainB"]

        # Setup directory structure
        (tmp_root / "resource" / "raw").mkdir(parents=True)
        (tmp_root / "resource" / "cleaned").mkdir(parents=True)
        (tmp_root / "resource" / "filtered" / "test_tag").mkdir(parents=True)
        (tmp_root / "resource" / "mapped" / "test_tag").mkdir(parents=True)
        (tmp_root / "resource" / "splits" / "test_tag").mkdir(parents=True)

        # Create synthetic review data
        for domain in domains:
            _create_synthetic_review_data(tmp_root / "resource" / "raw", domain)

        paths = get_data_paths(config={"paths": {
            "src_root": ".",
            "config_dir": "configs",
            "raw_dir": "resource/raw",
            "cleaned_dir": "resource/cleaned",
            "filtered_dir": "resource/filtered",
            "mapped_dir": "resource/mapped",
            "splits_dir": "resource/splits",
            "item_knn_dir": "resource/item_knn",
            "corpus_dir": "resource/corpus",
            "log_dir": "experiments/logs",
            "experiments_dir": "experiments",
        }}, tag="test_tag")
        paths = replace(paths,
            raw_dir=tmp_root / "resource" / "raw",
            cleaned_dir=tmp_root / "resource" / "cleaned",
            filtered_dir=tmp_root / "resource" / "filtered" / "test_tag",
            mapped_dir=tmp_root / "resource" / "mapped" / "test_tag",
            splits_dir=tmp_root / "resource" / "splits" / "test_tag",
        )

        # Run pipeline
        print("\n=== Running pipeline ===")
        for domain in domains:
            clean_domain(paths, domain, chunk_size=50, force=True)

        merge_filter_domains(paths, domains, force=True)

        map_split(paths, force=True)

        # Verify outputs exist and are valid
        print("\n=== Verifying outputs ===")

        # Stage 1 outputs
        for domain in domains:
            df = pd.read_parquet(paths.cleaned_path(domain))
            assert len(df) > 0
            assert set(df.columns) == {"user_id", "parent_asin", "rating", "timestamp"}
            assert df["rating"].dtype == "float32"
            print(f"Stage 1 {domain}: {len(df)} rows, OK")

        # Stage 2 output
        filtered = pd.read_parquet(paths.filtered_path)
        assert len(filtered) > 0
        assert "domain" in filtered.columns
        assert "original_item_id" in filtered.columns
        print(f"Stage 2 filtered: {len(filtered)} rows, OK")

        # Stage 3 outputs
        for split_name in ["train", "validation", "test"]:
            split = pd.read_parquet(paths.split_path(split_name))
            assert len(split) > 0
            assert "user_idx" in split.columns
            assert "item_idx" in split.columns
            print(f"Stage 3 {split_name}: {len(split)} rows, OK")

        # Mappings
        with open(paths.mapped_dir / "user2id.json", "r") as f:
            user2id = json.load(f)
        with open(paths.mapped_dir / "item2id.json", "r") as f:
            item2id = json.load(f)
        assert len(user2id) > 0
        assert len(item2id) > 0
        print(f"Mappings: {len(user2id)} users, {len(item2id)} items, OK")
```

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_pipeline_stages_independent():
    """Test that running individual stages produces same results as running all."""
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmpdir:
        tmp_root = Path(tmpdir)

        domains = ["DomainA", "DomainB"]

        # Setup
        (tmp_root / "resource" / "raw").mkdir(parents=True)
        (tmp_root / "resource" / "cleaned").mkdir(parents=True)
        (tmp_root / "resource" / "filtered" / "test_tag").mkdir(parents=True)
        (tmp_root / "resource" / "mapped" / "test_tag").mkdir(parents=True)
        (tmp_root / "resource" / "splits" / "test_tag").mkdir(parents=True)

        for domain in domains:
            _create_synthetic_review_data(tmp_root / "resource" / "raw", domain)

        paths = get_data_paths(config={"paths": {
            "src_root": ".",
            "config_dir": "configs",
            "raw_dir": "resource/raw",
            "cleaned_dir": "resource/cleaned",
            "filtered_dir": "resource/filtered",
            "mapped_dir": "resource/mapped",
            "splits_dir": "resource/splits",
            "item_knn_dir": "resource/item_knn",
            "corpus_dir": "resource/corpus",
            "log_dir": "experiments/logs",
            "experiments_dir": "experiments",
        }}, tag="test_tag")
        paths = replace(paths,
            raw_dir=tmp_root / "resource" / "raw",
            cleaned_dir=tmp_root / "resource" / "cleaned",
            filtered_dir=tmp_root / "resource" / "filtered" / "test_tag",
            mapped_dir=tmp_root / "resource" / "mapped" / "test_tag",
            splits_dir=tmp_root / "resource" / "splits" / "test_tag",
        )

        # Run stage 1 only
        for domain in domains:
            clean_domain(paths, domain, chunk_size=50, force=True)

        # Run stage 2 only
        merge_filter_domains(paths, domains, force=True)

        # Run stage 3 only
        map_split(paths, force=True)

        # Verify outputs exist
        for domain in domains:
            assert paths.cleaned_path(domain).exists()
        assert paths.filtered_path.exists()
        assert paths.split_path("train").exists()
        assert paths.split_path("validation").exists()
        assert paths.split_path("test").exists()
```

```python
def test_pipeline_fail_fast():
    """Test that pipeline fails fast with proper error messages."""
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmpdir:
        tmp_root = Path(tmpdir)

        (tmp_root / "resource" / "raw").mkdir(parents=True)
        (tmp_root / "resource" / "cleaned").mkdir(parents=True)
        (tmp_root / "resource" / "filtered" / "test_tag").mkdir(parents=True)
        (tmp_root / "resource" / "mapped" / "test_tag").mkdir(parents=True)
        (tmp_root / "resource" / "splits" / "test_tag").mkdir(parents=True)

        paths = get_data_paths(config={"paths": {
            "src_root": ".",
            "config_dir": "configs",
            "raw_dir": "resource/raw",
            "cleaned_dir": "resource/cleaned",
            "filtered_dir": "resource/filtered",
            "mapped_dir": "resource/mapped",
            "splits_dir": "resource/splits",
            "item_knn_dir": "resource/item_knn",
            "corpus_dir": "resource/corpus",
            "log_dir": "experiments/logs",
            "experiments_dir": "experiments",
        }}, tag="test_tag")
        paths = replace(paths,
            raw_dir=tmp_root / "resource" / "raw",
            cleaned_dir=tmp_root / "resource" / "cleaned",
            filtered_dir=tmp_root / "resource" / "filtered" / "test_tag",
            mapped_dir=tmp_root / "resource" / "mapped" / "test_tag",
            splits_dir=tmp_root / "resource" / "splits" / "test_tag",
        )

        # Missing input for stage 2 should raise FileNotFoundError
        with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError, match="Không tìm thấy"):
            merge_filter_domains(paths, ["DomainA"], force=True)
```

```python
def test_pipeline_k_core_blocked():
    """Test that k-core blocking still works."""
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmpdir:
        tmp_root = Path(tmpdir)

        (tmp_root / "resource" / "raw").mkdir(parents=True)
        (tmp_root / "resource" / "cleaned").mkdir(parents=True)
        (tmp_root / "resource" / "filtered" / "test_tag").mkdir(parents=True)
        (tmp_root / "resource" / "mapped" / "test_tag").mkdir(parents=True)
        (tmp_root / "resource" / "splits" / "test_tag").mkdir(parents=True)

        # Create minimal data that will be filtered out by k-core
        domain = "DomainA"
        path = tmp_root / "resource" / "raw" / f"{domain}.jsonl.gz"
        with gzip.open(path, "wt", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            for i in range(2):  # Very sparse data
                f.write(json.dumps({
                    "user_id": f"u{i}",
                    "parent_asin": f"i{i}",
                    "rating": 5.0,
                    "timestamp": 1000 + i * 10,
                    "title": f"Title {i}",
                }) + "\n")

        paths = get_data_paths(config={"paths": {
            "src_root": ".",
            "config_dir": "configs",
            "raw_dir": "resource/raw",
            "cleaned_dir": "resource/cleaned",
            "filtered_dir": "resource/filtered",
            "mapped_dir": "resource/mapped",
            "splits_dir": "resource/splits",
            "item_knn_dir": "resource/item_knn",
            "corpus_dir": "resource/corpus",
            "log_dir": "experiments/logs",
            "experiments_dir": "experiments",
        }}, tag="test_tag")
        paths = replace(paths,
            raw_dir=tmp_root / "resource" / "raw",
            cleaned_dir=tmp_root / "resource" / "cleaned",
            filtered_dir=tmp_root / "resource" / "filtered" / "test_tag",
            mapped_dir=tmp_root / "resource" / "mapped" / "test_tag",
            splits_dir=tmp_root / "resource" / "splits" / "test_tag",
        )

        clean_domain(paths, domain, chunk_size=50, force=True)

        # Stage 2 should exit with BLOCKED message
        with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="BLOCKED"):
            merge_filter_domains(paths, [domain], force=True)
```

```python
def test_experiment_config_serialization():
    """Test ExperimentConfig serialization round-trip."""
    config = ExperimentConfig(
        name="test_exp",
        description="Test experiment",
        agent_config={"model": "gpt-4"},
        evaluation_config={"layers": [1, 2, 3]},
        dataset={"path": "data/test.jsonl"},
        repetition_count=3,
        random_seed=42,
        tags=["test"],
    )

    data = config.to_dict()
    restored = ExperimentConfig.from_dict(data)

    assert restored.name == config.name
    assert restored.description == config.description
    assert restored.agent_config == config.agent_config
    assert restored.repetition_count == config.repetition_count
```

```python
def test_campaign_config_serialization():
    """Test CampaignConfig serialization round-trip."""
    campaign = CampaignConfig(
        campaign_id="test_campaign",
        description="Test campaign",
        dataset={"path": "data/test.jsonl"},
        experiments=[
            ExperimentConfig(name="exp1", description="Experiment 1"),
            ExperimentConfig(name="exp2", description="Experiment 2"),
        ],
        output_dir="experiments/test",
        comparison_pairs=[("exp1", "exp2")],
        statistical_settings={"confidence_level": 0.95},
    )

    data = campaign.to_dict()
    restored = CampaignConfig.from_dict(data)

    assert restored.campaign_id == campaign.campaign_id
    assert len(restored.experiments) == 2
    assert restored.comparison_pairs == campaign.comparison_pairs
```

```python
def test_campaign_config_hash():
    """Test campaign hash computation."""
    campaign1 = CampaignConfig(
        campaign_id="test_campaign",
        dataset={"path": "data/test.jsonl"},
        experiments=[
            ExperimentConfig(name="exp1", agent_config={"model": "gpt-4"}),
        ],
    )

    campaign2 = CampaignConfig(
        campaign_id="test_campaign",
        dataset={"path": "data/test.jsonl"},
        experiments=[
            ExperimentConfig(name="exp1", agent_config={"model": "gpt-4"}),
        ],
    )

    # Same config should produce same hash
    assert campaign1.compute_hash() == campaign2.compute_hash()

    # Different config should produce different hash
    campaign3 = CampaignConfig(
        campaign_id="test_campaign",
        dataset={"path": "data/test.jsonl"},
        experiments=[
            ExperimentConfig(name="exp1", agent_config={"model": "gpt-3.5"}),
        ],
    )

    assert campaign1.compute_hash() != campaign3.compute_hash()
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/campaign/test_campaign.py::test_campaign_runner_simulation`  (0.0168s)

_Test CampaignRunner orchestration with a stubbed _run_experiment (no subprocess)._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_campaign_runner_simulation(tmp_path: Path):
    """Test CampaignRunner orchestration with a stubbed _run_experiment (no subprocess)."""
    campaign = CampaignConfig(
        campaign_id="test_campaign",
        dataset={"path": "data/test.jsonl"},
        experiments=[
            ExperimentConfig(
                name="exp1",
                description="Experiment 1",
                repetition_count=2,
            ),
            ExperimentConfig(
                name="exp2",
                description="Experiment 2",
                repetition_count=1,
            ),
        ],
        output_dir=str(tmp_path / "campaign_output"),
        comparison_pairs=[("exp1", "exp2")],
    )

    original_run = CampaignRunner._run_experiment

    def fake_run(self, experiment, run_number, output_dir):
        return ExperimentRun(
            experiment_name=experiment.name,
            run_number=run_number,
            output_dir=str(output_dir / experiment.name / f"run_{run_number}"),
            success=True,
            metrics={"task_success_rate": 0.8},
        )

    CampaignRunner._run_experiment = fake_run

    try:
        runner = CampaignRunner(campaign, git_sha="test_sha")
        result = runner.run_campaign()
    finally:
        CampaignRunner._run_experiment = original_run

    assert result.campaign_id == campaign.campaign_id
    assert len(result.runs) == 3  # 2 runs for exp1 + 1 run for exp2
    assert result.summary["total_runs"] == 3
    assert result.summary["successful_runs"] == 3
    assert "exp1_vs_exp2" in result.comparisons
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/campaign/test_campaign.py::test_campaign_runner_with_failed_experiment`  (0.0037s)

_Test CampaignRunner handles failed experiments._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_campaign_runner_with_failed_experiment(tmp_path: Path):
    """Test CampaignRunner handles failed experiments."""
    campaign = CampaignConfig(
        campaign_id="test_campaign",
        dataset={"path": "data/test.jsonl"},
        experiments=[
            ExperimentConfig(
                name="exp_success",
                description="Successful experiment",
                repetition_count=1,
            ),
            ExperimentConfig(
                name="exp_fail",
                description="Failing experiment",
                repetition_count=1,
            ),
        ],
        output_dir=str(tmp_path / "campaign_output"),
    )

    # Override _run_experiment (the per-experiment unit) so no subprocess runs
    original_run = CampaignRunner._run_experiment

    def fake_run(self, experiment, run_number, output_dir):
        success = experiment.name != "exp_fail"
        return ExperimentRun(
            experiment_name=experiment.name,
            run_number=run_number,
            output_dir=str(output_dir / experiment.name / f"run_{run_number}"),
            success=success,
            error=None if success else "Simulated failure",
            metrics={"task_success_rate": 0.9} if success else {},
        )

    CampaignRunner._run_experiment = fake_run

    try:
        runner = CampaignRunner(campaign)
        result = runner.run_campaign()

        assert result.summary["total_runs"] == 2
        assert result.summary["successful_runs"] == 1
        assert result.summary["failed_runs"] == 1
    finally:
        CampaignRunner._run_experiment = original_run
```

```python
def test_checkpoint_manager(tmp_path: Path):
    """Test CheckpointManager save and load."""
    result = CampaignResult(
        campaign_id="test_campaign",
        campaign_hash="abc123",
        runs=[
            ExperimentRun(
                experiment_name="exp1",
                run_number=1,
                output_dir="experiments/exp1/run_1",
                success=True,
            ),
        ],
    )

    manager = CheckpointManager(tmp_path)
    manager.save_checkpoint(result)

    loaded = manager.load_checkpoint()

    assert loaded is not None
    assert loaded.campaign_id == result.campaign_id
    assert len(loaded.runs) == 1
```

```python
def test_checkpoint_manager_clear(tmp_path: Path):
    """Test CheckpointManager clear."""
    result = CampaignResult(
        campaign_id="test_campaign",
        campaign_hash="abc123",
        runs=[],
    )

    manager = CheckpointManager(tmp_path)
    manager.save_checkpoint(result)

    assert manager.checkpoint_file.exists()

    manager.clear_checkpoint()

    assert not manager.checkpoint_file.exists()
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/campaign/test_campaign.py::test_experiment_comparison`  (0.0251s)

_Test experiment comparison logic._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_experiment_comparison():
    """Test experiment comparison logic."""
    runs_a = [
        ExperimentRun(
            experiment_name="exp_a",
            run_number=1,
            output_dir="exp_a/run_1",
            success=True,
            metrics={"task_success_rate": 0.80, "latency": 1.0},
        ),
        ExperimentRun(
            experiment_name="exp_a",
            run_number=2,
            output_dir="exp_a/run_2",
            success=True,
            metrics={"task_success_rate": 0.85, "latency": 1.1},
        ),
    ]

    runs_b = [
        ExperimentRun(
            experiment_name="exp_b",
            run_number=1,
            output_dir="exp_b/run_1",
            success=True,
            metrics={"task_success_rate": 0.90, "latency": 0.9},
        ),
    ]

    runner = CampaignRunner(CampaignConfig(campaign_id="test"))
    comparison = runner._compare_experiments(runs_a, runs_b)

    assert comparison["status"] == "success"
    assert comparison["experiment_a"] == "exp_a"
    assert comparison["experiment_b"] == "exp_b"
    assert comparison["runs_a"] == 2
    assert comparison["runs_b"] == 1

    # Check task_success_rate delta
    tsr_delta = comparison["deltas"]["task_success_rate"]
    assert tsr_delta["a"] == pytest.approx(0.825)  # Average of 0.80 and 0.85
    assert tsr_delta["b"] == 0.90
    assert tsr_delta["delta"] == pytest.approx(0.075)
```

```python
def test_campaign_example_yaml_loads():
    """Test that the example campaign YAML can be loaded."""
    example_path = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "campaign" / "example_campaign.yaml"

    if example_path.exists():
        campaign = CampaignConfig.from_yaml(example_path)

        assert campaign.campaign_id == "campaign_001_baseline_vs_improved"
        assert len(campaign.experiments) == 4
        assert len(campaign.comparison_pairs) == 5
        assert campaign.statistical_settings["confidence_level"] == 0.95
```

```python
def test_build_config_overlay_maps_known_keys():
    """_build_config_overlay maps agent_config/evaluation_config/random_seed into overlay shape."""
    runner = CampaignRunner(CampaignConfig(campaign_id="camp_x"))
    exp = ExperimentConfig(
        name="exp_a",
        agent_config={"agents": ["baseline"], "inference_mode": "template", "reflect_rounds": 2},
        evaluation_config={"per_segment": 5},
        random_seed=7,
    )
    ov = runner._build_config_overlay(exp, 3)

    ae = ov["agent/agent_eval"]["agent_eval"]
    assert ae["agents"] == ["baseline"]
    assert ae["per_segment"] == 5
    assert ae["trajectory_file"] == "camp_x_exp_a_run3_trajectories.jsonl"
    assert ov["agent/inference"]["inference"]["mode"] == "template"
    assert ov["agent/agent"]["agent"]["reflect_rounds"] == 2
    assert ov["eval/baselines"]["baselines"]["seed"] == 7
```

```python
def test_build_config_overlay_uses_campaign_defaults():
    """Defaults (agents, allow_missing_corpus_sources) come from CampaignConfig, not literals."""
    runner = CampaignRunner(CampaignConfig(
        campaign_id="c",
        default_agents=["planner"],
        allow_missing_corpus_sources=False,
    ))
    ov = runner._build_config_overlay(ExperimentConfig(name="e"), 1)
    ae = ov["agent/agent_eval"]["agent_eval"]
    assert ae["agents"] == ["planner"]
    assert ae["allow_missing_corpus_sources"] is False
```

```python
def test_build_config_overlay_rejects_unmapped_keys():
    """Unknown agent_config/evaluation_config keys raise instead of being dropped."""
    runner = CampaignRunner(CampaignConfig(campaign_id="c"))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="unmapped config keys"):
        runner._build_config_overlay(ExperimentConfig(name="e", agent_config={"bogus": 1}), 1)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="unmapped config keys"):
        runner._build_config_overlay(ExperimentConfig(name="e", evaluation_config={"bogus": 1}), 1)
```

__load_experiment_metrics parses 'saved -> <dir>' and flattens metrics.json._

```python
def test_load_experiment_metrics_from_stdout(tmp_path: Path):
    """_load_experiment_metrics parses 'saved -> <dir>' and flattens metrics.json."""
    exp_dir = tmp_path / "exp_042"
    exp_dir.mkdir()
    (exp_dir / "metrics.json").write_text(json.dumps({
        "k": 10,
        "summary": {"planner": {"similar": {"all": {"task_success": 0.9, "n": 20}}}},
    }), encoding="utf-8")

    runner = CampaignRunner(CampaignConfig(campaign_id="c"))
    metrics = runner._load_experiment_metrics(f"some output\nsaved -> {exp_dir}\n")
    assert metrics["planner_similar_task_success"] == 0.9

    # No 'saved ->' line or missing file -> empty dict, not an exception
    assert runner._load_experiment_metrics("no save line") == {}
    assert runner._load_experiment_metrics(f"saved -> {tmp_path / 'nope'}") == {}
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/campaign/test_campaign.py::test_auto_comparison_pairs_are_actually_used`  (0.0035s)

_parameter_grid + empty comparison_pairs -> auto-generated pairs must reach _run_comparisons._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_auto_comparison_pairs_are_actually_used(tmp_path: Path):
    """parameter_grid + empty comparison_pairs -> auto-generated pairs must reach _run_comparisons."""
    campaign = CampaignConfig(
        campaign_id="grid_campaign",
        dataset={"path": "data/test.jsonl"},
        parameter_grid={"inference_mode": ["template", "llm"]},
        comparison_pairs=[],
        output_dir=str(tmp_path / "out"),
    )

    original_run = CampaignRunner._run_experiment

    def fake_run(self, experiment, run_number, output_dir):
        return ExperimentRun(
            experiment_name=experiment.name,
            run_number=run_number,
            output_dir=str(output_dir / experiment.name / f"run_{run_number}"),
            success=True,
            metrics={"task_success_rate": 0.8},
        )

    CampaignRunner._run_experiment = fake_run
    try:
        result = CampaignRunner(campaign).run_campaign()
    finally:
        CampaignRunner._run_experiment = original_run

    assert result.comparisons, "auto-generated comparison pairs were dropped"
    (pair_key,) = result.comparisons.keys()
    assert pair_key.endswith("_vs_" + pair_key.split("_vs_")[-1])
    assert result.comparisons[pair_key]["status"] == "success"
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/campaign/test_campaign.py::test_statistical_settings_produce_ci_and_paired_delta`  (0.0102s)

_statistical_settings from campaign.yaml drives bootstrap CIs (Phase 4.1)._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_statistical_settings_produce_ci_and_paired_delta():
    """statistical_settings from campaign.yaml drives bootstrap CIs (Phase 4.1)."""
    cfg = CampaignConfig(
        campaign_id="stat_test",
        statistical_settings={
            "confidence_level": 0.95,
            "bootstrap_samples": 200,
            "paired_comparison": True,
            "random_seed": 7,
        },
    )
    runner = CampaignRunner(cfg)
    runs_a, runs_b = _stat_runs()
    comparison = runner._compare_experiments(runs_a, runs_b)

    assert comparison["statistics"]["confidence_level"] == 0.95
    assert comparison["statistics"]["bootstrap_samples"] == 200

    tsr = comparison["deltas"]["task_success_rate"]
    # Paired equal-length runs → delta CI + p_value present
    assert "delta_ci" in tsr and "p_value" in tsr and "significant" in tsr
    # B is consistently ~0.10 above A → CI excludes 0 → significant
    assert tsr["significant"] is True
    assert tsr["delta_ci"][0] > 0

    # Per-experiment mean/std/CI across runs
    stats = comparison["metric_stats"]["task_success_rate"]
    assert stats["a"]["n"] == 3 and stats["b"]["n"] == 3
    assert stats["a"]["ci"][0] <= stats["a"]["mean"] <= stats["a"]["ci"][1]
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/campaign/test_campaign.py::test_unpaired_or_disabled_statistics_skip_delta_ci`  (0.0075s)

_paired_comparison=false or unequal run counts → no delta_ci._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_unpaired_or_disabled_statistics_skip_delta_ci():
    """paired_comparison=false or unequal run counts → no delta_ci."""
    cfg = CampaignConfig(
        campaign_id="stat_test2",
        statistical_settings={"paired_comparison": False, "bootstrap_samples": 100},
    )
    runner = CampaignRunner(cfg)
    runs_a, runs_b = _stat_runs()
    comparison = runner._compare_experiments(runs_a, runs_b)
    assert "delta_ci" not in comparison["deltas"]["task_success_rate"]

    # Unequal lengths with paired enabled → also no delta_ci
    cfg2 = CampaignConfig(
        campaign_id="stat_test3",
        statistical_settings={"paired_comparison": True, "bootstrap_samples": 100},
    )
    runner2 = CampaignRunner(cfg2)
    comparison2 = runner2._compare_experiments(runs_a, runs_b[:2])
    assert "delta_ci" not in comparison2["deltas"]["task_success_rate"]
```

```python
def test_repo_campaign_yaml_still_loads_via_config_system():
    """Regression: configs/evaluation/campaign.yaml parses identically through load_config."""
    from package.config import CONFIG_DIR

    campaign = CampaignConfig.from_yaml(CONFIG_DIR / "evaluation" / "campaign.yaml")

    assert campaign.campaign_id == "campaign_001_parameter_grid"
    assert campaign.statistical_settings["confidence_level"] == 0.95
    assert len(campaign.expand_parameter_grid()) == 12  # 3 agents x 2 modes x 2 reflect_rounds
```

```python
def test_campaign_config_rejects_unknown_keys(tmp_path):
    """configs/-style campaign files are schema-validated with extra=forbid."""
    from package.config.load_config import ConfigError, load_config

    cfg_dir = tmp_path / "evaluation"
    cfg_dir.mkdir()
    (cfg_dir / "campaign.yaml").write_text('campaign_id: "x"\nbogus_key: 1\n', encoding="utf-8")

    with pytest.raises(ConfigError):
        load_config("evaluation/campaign", config_dir=tmp_path)
```

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_campaign_config_overlay_is_honored(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """CampaignConfig.from_yaml routes configs/ paths through load_config (CONFIG_OVERLAY_PATH applies)."""
    from package.config import CONFIG_DIR

    overlay = tmp_path / "overlay.yaml"
    overlay.write_text(
        'evaluation/campaign:\n  output_dir: "agent_eval/campaigns/overlaid_test"\n',
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    monkeypatch.setenv("CONFIG_OVERLAY_PATH", str(overlay))

    campaign = CampaignConfig.from_yaml(CONFIG_DIR / "evaluation" / "campaign.yaml")

    assert campaign.output_dir == "agent_eval/campaigns/overlaid_test"
```
